## 2026-09-12 JST provider void-response repair — locally accepted, PR840 open

The user requested diagnosis and repair of retry2's `UNKNOWN`. Work continues in the same task with one primary, using `D:/V_streamer_tools/.worktrees/gate1-controller-provider-repair-20260912` / `codex/gate1-controller-provider-repair-20260912` from `fb1152d`. The official OpenAPI at [upstream commit26585dd](https://github.com/supabase/supabase/blob/26585dd4a4d6db8910a595214c9f6e8fdd206768/apps/docs/spec/api_v1_openapi.json) defines HTTP200 for pause/restore with no response content/schema. The previous documentation display of `{}` was incorrectly interpreted as a required JSON response body. The old provider reproduces `UNKNOWN` for both absent and zero-byte response streams, while accepting JSON `{}`. Native loopback HTTP and the actual Worker/SQLite path reproduce the erroneous transition to `CLOSING`. This confirms a response-contract defect; retry2's exact wire response is still unavailable because provider details were discarded and its deployed observability was disabled. It does not prove that every historical `UNKNOWN` had this cause.

The local fix accepts only complete HTTP200 zero-byte mutation responses or the existing JSON empty-object form. Emptiness is based on received byte count, so a UTF-8 BOM-only body is not accepted as empty. Non-200 responses, malformed/unexpected JSON, unfinished/failed streams, size limits and the three-second deadline still fail closed. Metadata binding, one-use claims, liveness, cleanup, credentials, public-state/predecessor schemas and old `UNKNOWN` records are unchanged. The regression tests failed before repair and passed afterward, including native HTTP framing and a local workerd/SQLite four-operation cycle with persistence and duplicate rejection. Final acceptance passed all99 checks in eight related suites, Worker ESLint, changed-module syntax, operator contract, disabled simulation dry-run and root diff review. Evidence is under `.tmp/gate1-provider-repair-20260912/`; the dry-run bundle is retained under `workers/gate1-recovery-controller/.tmp/gate1-controller-build-simulation/`. The old153 evidence files/101 inputs and retry2's156 evidence files/five protected files/111 fixed inputs still match their sealed digests.

After explicit authorization, the six accepted files were committed as `50d31334f4669b40b05f8b5e15ffae2f4daec9f4`, pushed, and published in [PR840](https://github.com/kurodev-web-tools/tool-portal/pull/840) against `codex/comment-translator-paid-v1-preview` at `fb1152d`. Published Git content matched the accepted files with only checkout CRLF normalization; no product source changed after the99 passing checks. Initial PR readback confirmed the six paths, exact base/head, mergeability and no registered checks; this is not a CI pass. Publication evidence is in `.tmp/gate1-provider-publication-20260912/`.

No merge, new PAT/OAuth, provider/project mutation, upload/deploy, trial or subagent occurred. The sealed actual retry2 terminal state still fails `predecessorStateText` because its Preview outcomes are `UNKNOWN`. Before a new external trial, an explicit transition design must retain those outcomes, consumed claims and evidence while binding independent safe-closure verification to the old run/source/state. ## 2026-09-12 JST explicit predecessor/source transition — locally accepted, unpublished

The user authorized continuing from the accepted clock repair in the same repair worktree at dbdbb45: `D:/V_streamer_tools/.worktrees/gate1-controller-clock-repair-20260912`, branch `codex/gate1-controller-clock-repair-20260912`. The implemented explicit predecessor binding contains the prior runId, prior sourceCommit and SHA256 of its canonical public state. A new client freezes this binding from its reviewed local packet, matches the initial state without adopting the old run's sequence/lease, and sends the same binding with its one new arm request. Empty history requires no binding; existing history requires one, including unchanged-source successors. Mutating the caller's binding after construction cannot alter the frozen authority. Missing, wrong or reused identity is rejected.

The Worker permits a sourceCommit change only across a compatible closed predecessor: mode, Preview/Recovery/Production roles, organization and emergency policy stay identical. Eligible states are ENDED_NO_MUTATION with zero claims, or RESTORED with completed containment claims; both require Preview ACTIVE_HEALTHY, Recovery INACTIVE and complete observation metadata. ARMED, CLOSING, NEEDS_OPERATOR, UNKNOWN/PENDING containment and malformed states are rejected. Read-only state projection may expose an eligible old-source predecessor under the new policy; it does not rewrite policy, renew liveness or claim formal stopping acceptance.

Before registering the successor, the Worker verifies the predecessor binding and recomputes its state digest. Inside one SQLite transaction it rechecks the exact captured state and retained used_runs row, validates the new input/deadline, rejects every previously used run ID, inserts the successor and updates the current slot. Old ledger bytes remain unchanged. An arm whose captured predecessor changed while hashing rejects before registration or provider I/O. New arm observations still read both projects freshly; clock/freshness, operation limits, independent formal proofs, single abort and no-retry rules remain in force.

The original missing-transition tests failed before implementation. Focused predecessor/successor checks then passed16/16. Final client/proof/core/provider/Worker/diagnostic/predecessor suites passed92 TAP checks (87 top-level plus5 subtests), without failures or skips. Real local workerd/SQLite covered both eligible phases, retained row bytes, reload/reused IDs, source-only policy changes, wrong bindings, concurrent arms, deliberate snapshot drift, ledger mismatch and rollback after registration/current-slot writes. An initial local harness fetch failure with two simultaneous Miniflare instances was isolated by separating the independent empty-history case; the final suites passed with file concurrency1 while their intentional arm/command races remained concurrent. Focused ESLint, source syntax checks, operator contract, disabled simulation dry-run bundle and primary diff review passed. These are local synthetic checks, with no external Supabase calls.

The exact captured clock replay still passes on the final client: old-client rejection and repaired-client acceptance at the original receipt time, matching the actual response digest. All153 old evidence files and101 frozen inputs remain unchanged. Current acceptance/replay receipts are in `.tmp/gate1-predecessor-acceptance-20260912/`; the dry-run emitted its Worker under `workers/gate1-recovery-controller/.tmp/gate1-predecessor-acceptance-20260912/build/`. The previous clock-only receipt remains historical and unchanged. Ten files are pending locally (three source, four tests and three records/docs), with no dependency/configuration change or Git publication. Single primary, no subagent/model change; configured Astra/low does not prove observed runtime reasoning.

This closes the local predecessor/source initialization repair. ## 2026-09-11 JST next-step preparation — decision record superseded by the approved implementation above

Following the request to proceed, fetch and current worktree inspection confirmed the same a782e64 source and only the two operational-record changes. At02:03UTC all35 published producer/dependency bytes still matched, Docker Engine29.7.2 was available, and the previous attempt ledger was ended with all four counters1. Fresh identity-matched project metadata again showed Production/Preview ACTIVE_HEALTHY and Recovery INACTIVE. The executing process has no SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN. No new Hosted operation, fixture, backup, runtime deployment or credential was created.

Code inspection distinguishes three scopes: the native watchdog's5-minute backup/10-minute pause/20-minute confirmation deadlines, each live observer's own cumulative stopping-evidence window, and the synthetic executor's bounded transaction. These are phase-specific bounds; none is a provider-side automatic expiry of a resumed project. The native runner calls session.disconnect() on parent loss while that runner is alive, and the rehearsal executor can call its supplied stop callback after an owned failure. The Dashboard/MCP-driven setup and a powered-off PC have no independent recovery owner. Extending a local timer or restarting an old observer cannot close that gap.

Two approaches are ready for a user decision. An independent external recovery controller can act after this PC or Codex stops, but introduces an external runtime, a restricted management credential and a new action owner. A separately available human operator can instead perform the documented emergency sequence from another device; this avoids those additions but provides no automatic deadline guarantee and requires explicit acceptance of that operational limitation. The current local-only arrangement does not supply either control.

For the external approach, the review candidate is a separate Cloudflare Worker with SQLite-backed Durable Object storage. It must not share or change the existing application Worker. [Cloudflare pricing](https://developers.cloudflare.com/durable-objects/platform/pricing/) documents SQLite Durable Objects on the Free plan, but the actual account's plan/quota and zero-cost condition remain unverified. [Alarms](https://developers.cloudflare.com/durable-objects/api/alarms/) can run without this PC and may be delivered repeatedly; the design therefore requires a persisted one-use operation claim before any management request. An alarm is not an exact-time or successful-provider-action guarantee.

The proposed controller has one durable owner for the four per-attempt operation counters; the interactive process must never race it with direct pause/resume calls. Before the first Preview pause, it must have accepted the exact Preview/Recovery identities, a fresh run/commit binding, reviewed absolute end and liveness limits, and preservation readiness. Genuine accepted progress may refresh liveness only within that fixed end; a disconnected client, duplicated heartbeat or old run cannot extend it. On interruption, stop further rehearsal work, request the one owned Recovery pause when applicable, and independently read the result. Emergency Preview resume conditions must be explicitly approved and tested; metadata-only containment must never become formal two-round stopping evidence or migration acceptance. Unknown target/state, uncertain mutation outcome, exhausted claim or storage failure permits no automatic mutation retry. No SQL, Auth/Data API configuration, application credentials, backup contents or Production action belongs in this controller.

[Supabase scoped PATs](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/platform/personal-access-tokens) can restrict projects and permissions, but are in gradual public-alpha rollout; availability for this account is UNKNOWN. The candidate requires a short-lived token scoped only to Preview and Recovery, with Project Settings read-write and no other permissions. That provider permission also includes actions such as project deletion, so it must not be described as a pause/resume-only token; the controller's exact method/path allowlist is a separate defense. Classic full-account tokens are not a fallback. Credential creation, external secret storage, deployment/activation and a new Hosted attempt require their own concrete authorization; none follows from this design candidate.

Before implementation, decide between the external and human-operated paths and approve the chosen behavior. External acceptance must cover client disappearance, machine-off simulation, duplicate alarm/heartbeat, uncertain request completion, consumed counters, stale/wrong run, Production and all unbound-target rejection, and role-specific operation restrictions. First verify those cases locally with no remote calls, then independently verify the separately authorized deployed controller using synthetic control state before another Hosted rehearsal. Formal service capability/configuration/transfer acceptance remains pending. This is a decision draft, not an implementation or deployment approval; Gate1 remains NO-GO.

## 2026-09-11 JST R7 — synthetic transfer, execution guards and combined local verification

The user requested the next local preparation step at14:06UTC on September10. The same2d79 worktree and primary agent were used; no new Hosted pause/resume/configuration/import allowance was consumed. The current change is17 files including the earlier R5/R6 documentation and three R6 preparation scripts. Git publication, merge, deployment, Production work and retained-data Hosted restoration remain separately gated.

The new synthetic transfer accepts exactly three API-created example.test accounts and nine fixed Auth/account tables. It checks the managed Auth shape, every imported column and complete row, an empty destination for those tables, and the destination's whole-state fingerprint. Import, the shared credential reset and exact reset-delta comparison occur in one transaction. Other rows, sequences, definitions and security are preserved; migration history, grants and caller SQL are not imported. This is a synthetic rehearsal path, not a replacement for the formal six-artifact retained restore.

The executing adapter verifies PostgreSQL17, normal postgres role and database, a fresh fixture and the bound baseline before taking a durable one-use claim. It independently reads committed state afterward. The prepared Managed invocation fixes the approved Recovery reference, excludes both protected original projects, uses Direct5432/postgres with TLS verify-full and the pinned CA, disables psql startup files, and bounds connection/statement/output time and size. It accepts no connection override or arbitrary SQL. A claimed failure or uncertain result calls the supplied Recovery stop hook; a losing claim never stops another attempt. Claims are shared through Git common metadata and are never automatically removed. The stop hook still requires the separately approved, exact Recovery pause plus fresh independent stop observation; a boolean transport result by itself is not Hosted stopping evidence.

Configuration plans capture minimal changed fields and an exact inverse. They validate exact Management/Dashboard target URLs, PATCH method, known fields and duplicate-free JSON. Dashboard companion fields are allowed only when unchanged. The Management controller compares a fresh before snapshot, sends one PATCH, then separately reads back both the selected fields and every unmodified field of the full response, keeping secrets in process memory. Unexpected responses, redirects, changes or missing fields stop the owned attempt; there is no automatic retry/inverse. Canonical claim identity prevents JSON key order from creating another allowance. These factories are inert until invoked and return no stage authority.

Two local counterexamples are retained rather than promoted:

- PostgREST14.14 treats an empty db-schemas value as the public default. Hosted Studio sends Management db_schema="" for disablement, but those operations are not equivalent to setting the native role variable to an empty string. The local contract therefore closes its live PostgREST process using a verified absent-schema sentinel. The future Hosted run must independently verify its real config and complete503/PGRST002 REST/RPC/GraphQL responses before import. The native sentinel is never a Hosted payload. Sources: [pinned PostgREST parser](https://github.com/PostgREST/postgrest/blob/v14.14/src/PostgREST/Config.hs), [Studio Data API toggle](https://github.com/supabase/supabase/blob/master/apps/studio/components/interfaces/Settings/API/DataApiEnableSwitch.tsx).
- Creating a temporary observation function advances graphql.seq_schema_version through the GraphQL DDL event trigger, even when rolled back. The new observer uses an anonymous block in a read-only transaction and creates no objects; the new transfer also creates no temporary objects. Native before/after fingerprints now match with GraphQL enabled. The existing retained-restore SQL remains byte-identical for the checked input and retains its existing scope; this rehearsal does not requalify that older temporary-DDL path for a newly enabled GraphQL configuration. Source: [GraphQL schema-version trigger](https://github.com/supabase/pg_graphql/blob/master/sql/schema_version.sql).

Final combined native result: PASS. Two independently initialized databases use different inspected Auth signing keys; Realtime control metadata has its own local database while its tenant points to the destination application database. Startup/configuration/positive controls settle before short-lived source credentials are issued. Auth runs closed during import. The result includes32 Auth denials,20 Data API route probes and56 Storage object-route probes across the closed and Auth-reopened stages, with whole destination state unchanged across both service matrices. Storage empty listing/removal responses200/[] are explicitly recorded as EMPTY_NO_ROWS, not as denials or successful writes; nonempty results or any state delta fail. Original passwords/IDs work for all3 users; all six profile/quota rows match in every column. Only then does PostgREST reopen, followed by six owner reads, six cross-owner empty reads, three old-JWT rejections and anonymous rejection. These are account-foundation Free fixtures, not full application entitlement/UI acceptance.

Realtime controls prove an existing socket and public Broadcast work, both HTTP Broadcast forms deliver202, suspension closes the existing socket, and new websocket/HTTP requests return403; the same closed probes pass after Auth reopens. Its five native publication members are realtime.messages date partitions, not application tables. Their catalog/data remain present and covered by target fingerprints; no publication, grant or policy was deleted to manufacture an empty inventory. ### Findings and alternatives

The observed Direct classification was local cancellation, not an operating-system ETIMEDOUT or a server reply. Its upstream cause remains unknown. [RFC9293 section3.5.2](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9293.html#section-3.5.2) describes a TCP endpoint generating RST after receiving a segment for a nonexistent connection; it does not require a deprovisioned endpoint or an intervening network to return RST. The [Supabase pause endpoint](https://supabase.com/docs/reference/api/v1-pause-a-project) acknowledges the pause request; [Get project](https://supabase.com/docs/reference/api/v1-get-project) returns project identity/status separately. Supabase's [540 definition](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/troubleshooting/http-status-codes#540-project-paused) explicitly identifies a paused project unable to process requests until the owner resumes it. These public contracts support a provider-reported pause policy, not independent proof that every Direct path is physically closed.

At the design-review baseline, the transport's per-socket timeout and the supervising engine's entire confirmStopped budget were both3000ms. Management/DNS-before consumed part of that same outer window, leaving no opportunity to complete a full Direct timeout and then obtain management/DNS-after. The separate Preview observer similarly exhausted its common signal before final DNS. Changing only ECONNREFUSED handling would therefore have been incomplete; v2 resolves this through the approved phased budget.

| Approach | Meaning and tradeoff | Recommendation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Provider-reported pause plus contradiction checks | Trust exact, fresh Management INACTIVE plus complete540 on both known routes; Direct success vetoes acceptance, while a fully observed local timeout remains connectivity UNKNOWN. This deliberately removes independent Direct refusal as a mandatory proof. | Explicitly adopted; local implementation accepted. |
| Preserve the current refusal-only policy | Keep the existing guarantee and NO-GO until the required refusal is actually supported. A longer wait is not known to produce a refusal. Repeating the same pause is not justified by current evidence. | Retain if independent Direct refusal is non-negotiable. |
| Observe a previously authenticated database connection being terminated | A TLS-bound connection could add diagnostic evidence, but one session can be terminated while the database remains active. [PostgreSQL17 pg_terminate_backend](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/functions-admin.html#FUNCTIONS-ADMIN-SIGNAL) explicitly terminates one backend session. | Supplemental evidence only; not sufficient as a replacement proof. No session termination is proposed for Hosted. |

Service-health errors/healthy=false are not a substitute for paused status. The [service-health API](https://supabase.com/docs/reference/api/v1-get-services-health) reports health rather than an independently attested global database shutdown. Generic unhealthy/error states, a lost connection, a DNS failure or a local timeout alone cannot authorize Recovery.

### Adopted policy and observable result

Adopt only through an explicit new `stopEvidencePolicy: "supabase-inactive-v2"`, bound into the reviewed policy, exact execution authorization, source identity and durable receipts. Missing/old policy values must not select it implicitly. Emit a distinct `SOURCE_PAUSED_VERIFIED` result with that policy identity; update its supervising consumer deliberately. Do not relabel an old SOURCE_STATUS_UNKNOWN or old receipt as SOURCE_INACCESSIBLE. The new result means provider-reported pause with bounded independent client observations; it is not an independent physical-shutdown certificate. Control-plane error or stale/misreported provider status remains a residual trust risk that repeated samples cannot eliminate.

Preserve current preparation: selected published commit and producer-byte checks, private exact target/host/region binding, enabled bound publishable key, ACTIVE_HEALTHY identity, native PG17/read-only/verify-full TLS at ALL current Direct addresses, anon SELECT=false, exact healthy Auth and limited-table permission baseline, stable DNS, fresh same-process memory-only pins, protected backup and existing explicit one-pause authority. A failed preflight clears readiness and sends no pause. The native Production wrapper remains Production-only; Preview and a newly created Recovery require separate bound observers and their own route baseline.

Each confirmation round has three sequential phases: (1) independently fetch exact INACTIVE identity and the current Direct DNS set; (2) attempt every validated Direct address and fetch both known HTTP routes concurrently; (3) independently fetch exact INACTIVE identity and DNS again after those attempts have terminated. Both DNS observations must be completed; expected ENOTFOUND may be recorded as absent and contributes no stop proof. Any new/unvalidated address permanently invalidates the run. Other DNS errors are UNKNOWN. Both HTTP responses must be complete bounded540 responses from the fixed TLS origins without redirects; all other/mixed statuses, malformed transport results and incomplete/oversized bodies reject the round.

Direct results are typed separately. An address/port-matched ECONNREFUSED records explicit refusal. A socket that actually started, ran its full3000ms local observation window without connect, was destroyed, and reached its terminal close event records `NO_CONNECT_WITHIN_WINDOW`; its database-connectivity evidence remains UNKNOWN. Only those two terminal outcomes can accompany an otherwise complete provider-pause round. External cancellation, setup failure, missing addresses, identity-mismatched refusal, early abort, missing close and other network errors reject the round. Any observed TCP connect at a validated address after a round's initial INACTIVE is a contradiction and permanently blocks this run, even if HTTP still reports540. Late events cannot create acceptance; connect observed before terminal close must still veto a pending candidate.

Preserve the3000ms individual network-call bound, but explicitly change whole-round supervision from3000ms to12000ms: three phases of at most4000ms, including cancellation/closure handling, all under the single12000ms round deadline. Source verification and other overhead count toward that overall deadline. This makes room for final management/DNS readback after a full socket timeout; it does not extend an individual socket's observation window. Never accept a round after its parent deadline or signal cancellation.

Require TWO consecutive complete rounds under the same run, policy, source binding and pin set, with at least1000ms between rounds and at most30000ms from the first round's start to the second round's completion. An incomplete/unknown intervening round clears the consecutive count. Identity/source changes, new addresses or observed Direct connectivity permanently invalidate the run. Use real fresh requests for each round; do not combine cached fragments from separate failed rounds. Repeated samples reduce transient inconsistency but remain observations from the same provider, not separate shutdown authorities.

The original T0-based five-minute backup/readiness, ten-minute one-shot pause-request and twenty-minute final confirmed-pause/receipt limits remain unchanged. Monotonic/wall-clock maximum age, fail-closed clock handling, durable acknowledgement and no deadline reset on restart remain mandatory. Every phase/round is capped by the remaining absolute twenty-minute budget. Only two fully completed rounds and the durable final receipt within that budget can make the new policy's recovery eligibility true; whole Gate1 remains NO-GO until its other gates pass. Keep source paused after actual incident recovery; no automatic resume or deletion is added.

### Implementation and review scope

Primary agent implements in the existing worktree: versioned policy/authorization and runner validation; typed Direct outcomes and phased confirmation in the native transport; supervising round budget/consecutive-round state and distinct final evidence; existing watchdog/runner/operator contracts; and a separately bound ignored Preview observer. Update this document and task.md in the same cycle. Keep diagnostics to status, elapsed times, completeness, terminal classifications and aggregate counts. Never emit keys, raw responses, addresses, native stderr or private account identifiers. Dependency, database schema, application runtime and provider settings changes are unnecessary for this local implementation.

Review/test matrix: current active-baseline controls; old/missing policy rejection; two complete bound rounds with explicit refusals; two complete bound rounds with fully observed Direct timeouts but UNKNOWN Direct classification; active/pending/unknown or mismatched management status; missing/changed final management readback; wrong/mixed/incomplete HTTP; Direct open contradiction including late-connect ordering; new/missing/invalid address coverage; transient DNS errors versus completed ENOTFOUND; setup/early-cancel/close failures; no cross-round fragment reuse; consecutive-count reset; individual3000ms, phase4000ms, round12000ms and absolute5/10/20-minute boundary/cancellation cases; expired or unpersisted final evidence; unchanged one-pause/no-auto-resume constraints. Local tests and primary review cover this matrix; these results do not establish Hosted evidence.

Adoption and local implementation are approved and locally accepted. Publishing the reviewed six-file change and a new bounded Hosted pause/Recovery/resume packet remain separate; the previous run's exhausted allowance is not reset. Status: LOCAL_V2_IMPLEMENTATION_ACCEPTED / PUBLICATION_PENDING / HOSTED_V2_UNVERIFIED / Gate1 NO-GO.

An isolated HTTP Custom Access Token hook used a fixed synthetic operator ID and verified the pinned Auth service's webhook HMAC/timestamp. ### Decision prepared, not applied

Preserve the closed-restore condition and separate it from a deliberately declared public Auth reopening point. One possible revised P6 would keep Data API/application/providers for external business integrations closed, explicitly reopen only the required Auth provider after restoration acceptance, then perform the operator's login smoke before reopening business traffic. Signup and unused providers would remain closed. Existing users could nevertheless authenticate and mutate their own Auth state during that interval. This changes the current requirement for operator-only login BEFORE public Auth reopening; it needs explicit acceptance. It also needs independent confirmation that every business-data surface remains closed and a target-bound response to failed smoke. Turning Email off again does not invalidate tokens issued during the interval, so failure cannot be declared contained by toggling the provider alone; signing trust/session containment or target disposal must be independently supported and approved before Hosted execution.

The alternative is to retain operator-only pre-public Auth smoke as mandatory and continue searching for an enforceable mechanism; the tested HTTP hook combination is insufficient. No authentication bypass, admin impersonation link, all-user ban, password replacement, relaxed Auth-write allowance or new hosted architecture is selected. No support response or paid feature is a prerequisite for further local analysis.

The previous unsent support draft is replaced by this decision packet. Existing local replay acceptance is retained; all-HTTPS isolation remains the current requirement until a concrete revision is explicitly accepted. Gate1 NO-GO. This is design/source evidence, not a tested Hosted recipe.

### Proposed invariant and options

Candidate P2b would allow public HTTPS endpoints to remain reachable while preventing non-operator access to retained data, business-data writes, account creation, usable authentication issuance and recovery-triggered external messages before explicit reopening. Independent target signing material, protected credentials, existing atomic reset/preservation checks and source availability remain mandatory. Credential secrecy is not a substitute for denial controls: test with the target public key and a valid existing-user password. Invalid-request logs/rate counters may still change; this is a residual difference from total network isolation and requires an explicit bounded acceptance decision. No business/Auth preservation exception is implied.

| Option | Assessment |
| --- | --- |
| A: Recovery configuration closure + atomic reset | Preferred first experiment. Disable all enabled login providers, anonymous signup, signup, SSO/OAuth server/passkeys/third-party trust as applicable, and Data API; retain exact independent target keys. No temporary business RLS/ACL changes. Must test every alternative Auth path, outbound effect and the reopening transition; not yet sufficient for GO. |
| B: Add a Recovery-only Custom Access Token hook for operator smoke | Candidate only if A cannot preserve operator-only testing. Free plan offers this hook. It can return errors before issuance, but does not establish absence of earlier Auth writes, email sends or existing-token use. Hook failure/timeout must reject. Use an exact approved immutable operator ID, never user_metadata or a client-supplied role. Such an allowlist still relies on operator account credentials; it is not network isolation. |
| C: New self-hosted recovery service behind private ingress | Provides controllable network boundaries, but changes hosting/operations and potentially cost. Outside the current approved architecture; not selected. |

[Auth Hooks](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/auth/auth-hooks) lists Custom Access Token and Before User Created on Free/Pro; Password Verification Attempt is Teams/Enterprise and is excluded from the no-cost candidate. [Custom Access Token](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/auth/auth-hooks/custom-access-token-hook) is invoked before token issuance. These facts do not prove the complete candidate.

### Route coverage and unresolved evidence

| Surface | Proposed control / required test |
| --- | --- |
| Existing-user password and new signup | Email/phone provider off plus signup/anonymous off. Pinned [token.go](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/supabase/auth/v2.192.0/internal/api/token.go) checks provider state before password authentication. Prove valid-password denial and no account/session creation; signup-off alone is inadequate. |
| Magiclink, recovery, OTP, resend | Provider off. [magic_link.go](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/supabase/auth/v2.192.0/internal/api/magic_link.go) checks Email enabled; [api.go](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/supabase/auth/v2.192.0/internal/api/api.go) wraps recover with requireEmailProvider. Resend/OTP need separate native coverage, including no message or new token/flow residue. CAPTCHA/rate-limit rejection is not proof of provider denial. |
| Old access/refresh, verify GET/POST, PKCE, invite and email/phone changes | New target signing trust plus the already verified atomic invalidation. Provider-off is not a universal verify/PKCE guard. Validate rejection before/during/after commit with non-expired synthetic credentials and detect new sessions and unintended retained-row changes. Do not extract real-user tokens. |
| Social/SSO/passkey/Web3/third-party JWT and OAuth server | Independently enumerate actual target capabilities/trusted issuers and explicitly disable or prove absent. Unknown enabled capability stops execution; the historical email-only backup is not proof of current target configuration. |
| REST/GraphQL/RPC | Recovery Data API off with bound target readback and actual route probes. [API security](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/api/securing-your-api) documents Data API disablement independently of grants/RLS. Prove GraphQL and RPC coverage too; do not rewrite original grants to manufacture preservation. |
| Storage / Realtime / Functions | The earlier source inventory was empty, but refresh at the approved capture and assert empty target/restore inventories. Include public buckets/objects/signed URLs, publication tables, public Broadcast/Presence, Functions and existing sockets. Empty publication tables alone do not close Realtime. Any content or callable writer requires a separate control; Data API off does not cover these services. |
| App, callbacks, webhooks, scheduler, direct DB and background jobs | Recovery app stays operator-restricted across all hostnames; no provider/scheduler binding or success acknowledgements during recovery. Operator-only DB access is separate. Audit restored triggers/jobs/external connections and platform background activity. A denied Auth request can still produce logs; classify and verify instead of claiming all writes are impossible. |

### State transitions and preservation

EMPTY_CLOSED: independent target keys/trust and disabled entry points applied and independently checked before real data. No old target users, sessions or prior public use is acceptable. Capture the exact active-configuration field whitelist and inverse settings privately; never replay the raw242-field API response.

RESTORED_CLOSED: run the published atomic source-match/reset/delta/independent-readback path with the closure still active. Unknown capability, successful forbidden access, incomplete denial oracle, preservation mismatch or uncertain configuration fails closed; keep/dispose the owned target according to approved policy, never reopen on failure. Keep the original artifacts and cumulative incident deadlines.

OPERATOR_SMOKE: do not simply re-enable Email for everyone while the app proxy is closed; direct Auth remains reachable. A separately validated operator-only issuance boundary is required before enabling a provider for smoke. Option B must first prove non-operator denial and no forbidden side effects. A Postgres hook adds functions/ACLs covered by current source/security fingerprints; current restoration does NOT accept it automatically. Do not exclude the schema, weaken a hash or modify original backup files to pass. Resolve timing/declared target-only overlay and independently verify its removal in a separate reviewed change, or retain A as blocked at this transition.

REOPEN: only after closed restoration and authorized operator smoke pass, restore the exact approved settings and enable integrations in a reviewed order, record the public reopening point, and verify ordinary fresh login and intended account state. Fixture cleanup and expected smoke deltas must be explicit. No source shutdown or extra spending is added.

### Next bounded local evaluation

Use only cached Auth v2.192.0/PG images, a newly owned network=none synthetic target and local mail collector. Probe option A with valid fake passwords, old non-expired access/refresh/link/PKCE and direct resend/OTP calls while providers/signup are disabled, then after atomic reset. Assert exact error classes, session/token/flow/user preservation and local mail-count deltas. Include positive controls with providers enabled, otherwise a broken fixture could look safe. Stop and classify the first counterexample; no real backup or Hosted calls are needed. A positive result establishes local Auth closure only, not Hosted gateway/Storage/Realtime enforcement or operator smoke. Prototype B only after its fingerprint/timing design is reviewed.

A first bounded synthetic characterization used cached PG/Auth v2.192.0, a local mail collector and network=none. Positive-control password and recovery-PKCE generation succeeded before closure. With Email/phone/anonymous disabled and signup disabled, password422, recovery400, magiclink422, OTP422 and resend400 returned email_provider_disabled; signup422 returned signup_disabled. User/session/flow/one-time-token counts stayed unchanged for those six requests. This is count evidence only: row values, refresh-token rows, audit logs and mail-count deltas were not fully compared. With the same signing key and no reset, the old access token still returned200 and the old PKCE code issued a session200, proving provider closure alone is insufficient. The original successful atomic-reset evidence remains separate; this characterization did not combine the mechanisms. Owned containers/volumes0, no external delivery. Ignored receipt: .tmp/gate1-evidence-20260910/provider-closure-characterization.json. A timeout/error was not counted as a successful rejection.

No production implementation, support message, commit/push or Hosted operation occurred in this evaluation. Only the owned synthetic Auth configuration was changed and the target disposed. The user has approved investigation, not adoption of P2b or relaxation of Gate1. Remaining decision: validate option A locally; accept a precisely scoped P2 revision only once its closure and operator-smoke gaps are resolved.

## 2026-09-10 Approved disposable-target policy implemented locally

Status: LOCAL_DISPOSABLE_TARGET_VERIFIED / REAL_REPLAY_AND_HOSTED_UNACCEPTED. The user approved the design revision after the sequence-ownership finding. The earlier approval-pending and managed-sequence-blocked paragraphs below are historical checkpoints. No ownership grant, SET ROLE expansion, original-backup rewrite, remote deletion or production change was made.

The new explicit discard-target-v1 policy leaves the original Auth refresh sequence setter unchanged and omits its transactional RESTART guard only for auth.refresh_tokens_id_seq. All other sequence guards and the default strict path remain intact. Successful restoration still compares source state, applies the fixed credential reset and compares the full expected delta plus independent committed readback. This policy explicitly does not guarantee whole-state rollback: sequence residue after a failed transaction is expected, and that destination must never be reused or reopened.

Before the adapter sends SQL, an append-only ledger claims the immutable container ID and every mounted data-volume identity. Records are synced before execution and shared under Git common metadata across this repository's worktrees. Success, failure, normal resource removal and process death do not clear claims. Incomplete/corrupt records deny reuse. A replacement container cannot reuse a claimed volume; a fresh destination needs fresh volumes and a newly verified baseline. The ledger stores hashes/operational metadata, not SQL or credentials. Do not delete these records as temporary cleanup.

The ordinary-postgres adapter requires an owned exact image/baseline, network=none including actual attached networks, no published ports/host binds and a non-superuser session. Failures after acquiring the attempt report disposalRequired and stopConfirmed separately; stopping is not deletion. Unknown stopping remains unconfirmed and never accepts recovery. A losing/repeated claimant has no attempt ownership and must not stop or dispose another writer's target. Successful results remain RESTORED_QUARANTINED and targetReusable=false. External destruction still requires the exact target and separate authority; this API does not perform Hosted deletion or reopen services.

Verification: 45 related Node tests PASS; the ledger also survives a child-process SIGKILL and rejects reuse from a fresh process. Four native ordinary-postgres scenarios each use a fresh isolated container/volume: SQL failure, connection loss, committed-readback mismatch and successful Auth-setter restore/reset/readback. Failed cases demonstrate Auth sequence residue and retry rejection; the final fresh destination succeeds. All observed native fixture containers/volumes were removed, while claims remain. The existing strict native failure suite and cached Auth/TOTP regression also PASS, including old link/access/refresh rejection, original password/user ID login and same-factor fresh AAL2. Scoped lint covers all11 changed/new JavaScript files. The primary agent implements/reviews alone.

### Decision required before changing failure semantics

The current accepted design requires sequence state as well as row data to roll back. Keep that requirement unless the user approves a material revision. A concrete alternative is to preserve all original successful restore/reset/preservation checks and execute the original managed setter unchanged, while treating any failed or uncertain restore as a permanently unusable recovery destination: keep it isolated, never retry or reopen it, destroy it only under target-specific cleanup authority, and initialize a new destination before another attempt. Do not compensate by granting ownership or manually patching the failed sequence. This would replace complete sequence rollback with disposal of the failed destination; it is not yet implemented or approved.

That alternative requires proven P2 isolation before loading real data, explicit destination ownership/cleanup authority, and the existing overall5/10/20-minute budget without restarting its clock. Hosted isolation and target creation remain unresolved; healthy source pause, paid capacity and Hosted deletion are not authorized by this proposal. The other path is an independently supported owner-level sequence execution mechanism with reviewed permissions; none has been established. Until a choice is approved and verified, keep the existing failure rejection, do not run the real backup, and retain Gate1 NO-GO. No commit/push/PR or external mutation was performed in this continuation.

`scripts/lib/comment-translator-paid-core-v1-gate1-atomic-restore.mjs` validates the six original artifact names, order, byte lengths and SHA256 values without changing their contents. One explicit transaction restores them, compares the original sourceState on the same connection, checks the managed Auth shape and unsupported dependencies, computes the exact allowed post-reset projection, executes the fixed reset, compares every retained ordinary-table row and sequence value plus security/definition fingerprints, and commits. Successful process closure is followed by a separate connection comparing the committed fingerprint. Its result is explicitly LOCAL_SYNTHETIC_ONLY / stageAuthority=false / NO-GO. Verification entry points are `scripts/comment-translator-paid-core-v1-gate1-atomic-restore.test.mjs`, `scripts/comment-translator-paid-core-v1-gate1-atomic-native-contract.mjs`, and `scripts/comment-translator-paid-core-v1-gate1-atomic-auth-native-contract.mjs`. The native fixture covers success, source mismatch, mid-restore failure, connection loss, unexpected rule, a deliberately corrupted reset delta, timeout, cancellation, unknown flow and unknown managed column. The actual cached Auth fixture keeps Auth running while the restored rows remain uncommitted, observes old magiclink rejection before/after commit, rejects old access/refresh credentials, logs in with the original password/user ID, and verifies a fresh AAL2 challenge with the same enrolled TOTP factor. Credential values never appear in the result.

Limits remain explicit: real six-file backup replay and normal managed-postgres permissions are not accepted by these fixtures. COPY input and nontransactional sequence setters are rejected rather than rewritten; their compatibility with the real artifact set remains unresolved. No new source publication, original-backup modification, Production connection, restore, pause, migration, SMTP/SMS send or external setting change occurred. Published-producer integration, the full real-backup/catalog acceptance and P2 whole-Recovery HTTPS isolation remain open. Healthy Free stays online during final backup; the existing 20-minute RPO is unchanged. Gate1 remains NO-GO.

## 2026-09-09 Goal 3: bounded post-apply capture repair locally accepted, unpublished

Scope and design: the user authorized setting and executing the next local repair goal after Goal2. The measured post-apply query produced 1133757 bytes, so the former 1 MiB limit could not capture a complete catalog. Inspection also found the same limit in native psql normalization, the JSON parser and final artifact-reference validation. Use one exported POSTAPPLY_MAX_OUTPUT_BYTES = 4 MiB allowance across those boundaries. This gives bounded headroom above the measured catalog without introducing streaming/storage changes. An unrestricted increase was rejected; changing only the first reader would leave downstream failures.

The local Docker reader uses the shared limit for both spawn capture and UTF8 byte validation. The fixed collector opts into the same allowance through createPsqlTransport, whose optional numeric limit must be a positive safe integer no greater than 4 MiB. Its version probe and ordinary preflight default/parser stay at 1 MiB. Evidence-index validation and actual file reads allow 4 MiB only for catalog/catalog-expectations roles in previewReadback, rehearsalBackup, productionReadback and canonicalReadback. Other roles/stages, receipts and authority documents retain 1 MiB; the aggregate JSON allowance remains 32 MiB. PostgreSQL17's [official archiver implementation](https://github.com/postgres/postgres/blob/REL_17_STABLE/src/bin/pg_dump/pg_backup_archiver.c#L2285-L2298) leaves uncompressed plain-text stdout in Windows text mode; archive/compressed stdout uses binary mode. This explains the observed carriage-return insertion.

Do not send test email in this preparation |

### Writer coverage and boundary matrix

A HTTP verb filter or Translator allowlist is insufficient. Before opening a recovery endpoint, the incident operator must provide the exact deployed rule identifiers and coverage for all application hostnames including workers.dev/preview aliases, plus the independent Supabase endpoints and non-HTTP writers. No new access rule or database privilege change is approved by this checklist.

| Writer entry / source anchor | Possible effect | Required incident restriction and proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Account Server Actions: app/account/actions.ts and billing/actions.ts | Auth signup/signin/password/reset/logout, user_preferences upsert, YouTube connect/disconnect, Checkout/Portal | Cover Server Action POST requests on page URLs, not just /api. Block public application traffic before middleware/action execution; no Checkout or email test during isolation |
| Translator Server Actions: app/tools/comment-translator/actions.ts and session/feed/account/retention-waitlist-actions.ts | Start/stop/heartbeat, usage/feed persistence, retention/deletion, waitlist, credential refresh | Restrict every action entry and existing session poller; verify already-open tabs and in-flight/background work cannot continue writing after the accepted boundary |
| Session/harness APIs: app/api/comment-translator/session/route.ts and free-beta/route-api-harness/route.ts | Command dispatch and persistence | Include both routes and all accepted methods in edge coverage; keep harness/runtime gates closed |
| GET callbacks and credential-status: app/auth/confirm/route.ts, youtube/oauth/callback/route.ts, youtube/credential-status/route.ts; middleware.ts | OTP/code exchange, token persistence/refresh and Auth session work | Treat GET as potentially state-changing. Block callbacks and relevant page requests before application Auth middleware; ordinary page GET is not a safe isolation probe |
| Stripe webhook: billing/webhook/route.ts | Signed entitlement/lifecycle reconciliation | Block or use an approved provider-compatible deferred-delivery procedure; never acknowledge discarded events as success. Verify replay/idempotency and resulting entitlement state before reopening; provider changes/replay need their own authority |
| Maintenance/schedulers: paid-maintenance/route.ts and workers/comment-translator-paid-open-next-wrapper.mjs | GET-triggered reconciliation, outbound calls, scheduled writes | Keep database Cron and Cloudflare fallback inactive; inventory independent scheduler/manual invocation. Current Production has no fallback selector/Cron triggers, but current repository configuration differs |
| Direct Supabase clients: /auth/v1, /rest/v1 including RPC, /storage/v1, /realtime/v1 | Bypass application edge; Auth token refresh/signups, policy-authorized writes, service-role operations | Application hostname restrictions do not cover these endpoints. Require an approved supported isolation mechanism or keep recovery inaccessible/unpublished until ready. RLS does not block service-role holders. Do not widen/change grants to simulate a freeze |
| Direct DB/pooler, dashboard/CLI/SQL, CI/scripts and external service-role holders | Bypass HTTP edge and app gates; triggers execute with initiating writes | Inventory executing operators/jobs and credential references, quiesce approved writers and verify no in-flight writes. DB source pause remains the bounded-RPO source boundary; local tests cannot prove it |

### Ordered acceptance before incident execution

1. Finalize protected configuration/reference completeness, exact Production rebuild inputs, operator identity and hostname inventory. Confirm no unclassified writer or setting remains; a reference without retrievability/target compatibility is insufficient.
2. Review exact application-edge restrictions and independent Supabase/direct-DB isolation. Record owner, target, rule IDs, allowed operator, bypass-host coverage and rollback/reopening action. If the Free plan cannot support the required isolation, stop and revise the incident design under separate authority; do not assume an app allowlist covers Supabase.
3. Accept supervisor host/lifetime, immutable capture/executor receipt binding and actual paused-source oracle behavior. Keep the source healthy during preparation; no pause rehearsal is implied.
4. In an authorized incident, follow existing final-backup/arm/5-10-20-minute policy. Confirm source inaccessible before recovery provisioning; keep schedulers inactive, recovery unexposed and public callbacks/webhooks withheld. Restore and compare exact catalog/history/data/security, then privately read back all applicable configuration.
5. Under separately approved cutover/smoke authority, allow only the designated operator through every applicable boundary. Verify login/confirmation/CAPTCHA/email as specifically authorized, restored account identity, rejection of old credentials, retained independent decryption keys and route behavior. No real provider/session/Checkout exercise without its separate fixture/authority.
6. Recheck source remains paused, target state/canonical assertions, no unintended scheduler selection, and webhook/reconciliation disposition. Only then apply the reviewed reopening action with explicit authority. Observe new writes on Recovery, retain receipt evidence, and do not auto-unpause/delete the source.

Verification for this preparation is source/observation reconciliation plus operator-contract and diff checks; no live deny probe, login, webhook delivery or mutation was performed. Such probes can themselves create Auth/usage writes and require the approved incident test fixture. Additional sanitized observation is retained in ignored hosted-extra-auth-inventory.json. Unclosed execution gates: secure additional-settings preservation, exact enforceable isolation rules and writer/operator ownership, supervisor and actual pause oracle, recovery provision/restore/cutover. Preparation goal accepted; Gate1 remains NO-GO.

| 実tool | Docker Client/Server 29.7.2、Supabase CLI 2.109.0、PG client 17.11の確認済み記録あり |
| Preview-not-applied / production-not-applied | このGate 1実行では未適用。保存済みreadbackを現在状態と呼ばない |

現在のユーザー指示はサブエージェントの呼出し禁止。ユーザーが設定した現在の親モデルが実装・確認・受入れを直接担当する。以前のAstra/Luna分担は現在の実行指示ではない。runtime identityを独立確認したとは主張しない。単一writerを維持し、未コミット変更を保持する。

無償枠の制度根拠は[Supabase billing](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/platform/billing-on-supabase): Free project上限はOwner/Administratorの所属organizationを横断して2件、paused projectは算入しない。ただし健康なsourceを枠確保のため停止してはならない。APIで確認したplan/statusと、復旧先の無償確保・期限内復旧能力は別証拠である。

## Source / inventory / artifact境界

- 作業branchは `codex/comment-translator-paid-v1-gate1-source-readiness`。開始HEADは `2775fba1f5ca687d23b25c88286ec5125d1663ec`。公開前にactual diff全体をreviewし、commit/treeを実測値へ更新する。merge先は `codex/comment-translator-paid-v1-preview`。
- [environment inventories](../../scripts/fixtures/comment-translator-paid-core-v1-gate1-environment-inventories.json)の完全version/name行をauthorityとする。最終は56件、保存済み履歴からのpendingはPreview 26・Production 34。実行直前のCLI一覧・dry-runは完全行で再照合し、countだけでは進めない。
- `final55`はforward適用前の定義drift診断baseline。最終56件との差は承認済み `20260904000000_comment_translator_paid_gate1_a3_canonical_convergence` の1件だけ。欠落・重複・未知migrationは拒否する。
- desired canonicalはrepositoryのLF source bytesと安全なsecurity contractから生成する。[bridge states](../../scripts/fixtures/comment-translator-paid-core-v1-gate1-bridge-states.json)と[canonical authority](../../scripts/fixtures/comment-translator-paid-core-v1-gate1-canonical-authority.json)を完全構造で検証する。Previewはobserved driftであり、canonicalへコピーしない。
- canonicalのrowCountは観測値として分離する。legacyの0行条件、owner/ACL/RLS、完全FunctionRow、raw definitionMd5、dependencyは厳密比較を維持する。PUBLIC/anon/authenticatedのPaid RPC EXECUTEは0、service_roleは明示最小権限のみ。
- 81件は直接宣言されたRPC identityの件数。identity-only SHA-256を完全FunctionRow manifestのMD5へ代用しない。旧82件要求・旧MD5はretired。
- 保存済みProduction SHA-256は `569c325d2e09274f1b24b8352b482e8e38fcd3c94478b45824bfc7f8254bd3db`、Preview r13は `489b36c13953b9fa1f508ea7e8bdb9a6ca5cb0aa0998de07cbbbe4189d174a16`。r13は `C:/ProgramData/Codex/Gate1/catalog-readback-20260903-r13` に保持する。
- legacy SQL入力は `C:/ProgramData/Codex/Gate1/legacy-sql-20260902-r2` の4件のみ。本文を出力、repositoryへコピー、変更、削除しない。取得artifactとr4〜r12も保持する。digest再確認は新しいremote readbackではない。

## 実行順序と共通preflight

1. branch/HEAD/actual diff、適用するdesign/plan、writer、必要toolとinputを実行する同一processで確認する。
2. source契約・実DB/Vault検証・reviewを完了する。未実装・未実行・SETUP_BLOCKEDをPASSに変換しない。対象外差分、package/lock変更、秘密値・private identifier混入を検査する。
3. review済みsourceのcommit/push/PR、CI、Preview統合lineへのmergeを確認する。作業branchの存在やlocal commitだけではsourceCommit要件を満たさない。
4. Previewのfresh preflight、exact pending、適用、final56とcanonicalの完全readbackを確認する。既存inactive schedulerとVault値を変更しない。失敗中はProduction backup/DDLへ進まない。
5. DB/TLS、無償recovery capacity、rehearsal backup/restore、watchdogとcutover準備を実証する。
6. final snapshot backupとchecksumを完了し、arm後にProductionのexact pendingを適用する。完全history/catalog/security readbackが成功するまでVault/Cronへ進まない。
7. Vaultの2名を原子的に作成・count readbackし、その後にCronを原子的にinactiveで作成する。10分観測とfallback確認を完了する。
8. 全GO条件をactual evidenceで再監査する。単なるJSONのPASSラベル、synthetic結果、保存fixture、未実装verifierはGO根拠にならない。

Direct接続はprivateなtarget bindingのSHA-256、CAファイルのSHA-256、host/user/database/portの完全一致を必要とする。`verify-full`、PostgreSQL 17、`PGGSSENCMODE=disable`を維持する。接続値はsecure inputからtask process内へ渡し、chat・history・argv・stdoutへ出さない。CA未取得、binding不一致、秘密値表示が必要な経路では停止する。

read-only取得は固定query、REPEATABLE READ / READ ONLY、default transaction read-only、bounded timeout/output、最後のROLLBACKを使用する。適用前の22/30件契約と適用後56件契約を混同しない。Productionのcanonical wrapper欠落を空配列で補完しない。完全metadataはrestricted artifact内で検証し、外へはsanitized scalarだけを出す。

## 操作scope登録票

各行の実行記録には、private target binding、review済みsource revision、入力artifact digest、preflight結果、実施時刻、結果、停止時対応を結び付ける。値自体は公開しない。以下はcopy-readyなscope欄であり、空の証拠欄を自動的に承認済み結果へ変換しない。

| Scope | 許される操作と実行前条件 |
| --- | --- |
| source-implementation | plan allowlist内のsource・契約・docs。単一writer、既存差分保持、独立reviewが必要 |
| tooling-setup | 既存tool/imageの再利用を優先。追加費用は禁止。導入をPASSと推定せずversion/digest確認 |
| preview-apply | fresh一覧がexact pendingと一致し、source review/merge後のみ。scheduler/Vault/activation除外 |
| recovery-cost-capacity | plan・枠・region・PG17復旧先の無償確保を実証。有料project/PITR/IPv4/add-onは禁止 |
| recovery-project-provisioning | 確定済み無償枠とexact対象でのみ新規復旧先を作成。枠を空けるため本番を停止しない |
| recovery-region-extensions-auth | 復旧先のregion/extensions/Auth provider・redirectをreview済み構成と照合 |
| rehearsal-backup-restore | Preview gate後、Production read-only backupから新規隔離local Supabase PG17へrestore |
| final-backup | 同一exported snapshotに結び付くschema/data/historyと別rolesをrestricted保存 |
| bounded-rpo-watchdog | RPO最大20分・緊急停止/cutoverの条件を記録。実pause完了を証明できなければarm禁止 |
| production-migration-apply | backup/checksum、rehearsal、無償復旧能力、exact pendingが全部揃ってからarm・適用 |
| vault-write | schema/security readback後、予約2名だけ。rotation/update/decrypted read除外 |
| inactive-cron-configuration | Vault確認後、予約1jobだけをcommit前にinactive。activation除外 |
| incident-source-pause | arm後の失敗・deadlineに対するexact source project pauseだけ。健康なsourceの事前停止は禁止 |
| pause-confirmation | APIの要求成功だけではなく、deadline内の実際のinaccessibilityを確認 |
| recovery-restore | pause確認がT0+20分以内の場合だけ、新規復旧先へfinal backupをrestore。source上書き禁止 |
| endpoint-credential-cutover | 検証済み復旧先へ必要最小限の緊急endpoint/credential切替。通常deployと区別 |
| client-reauthentication | emergency cutoverに伴う再認証。秘密値を証拠へ含めない |
| reopening-writes | 復旧readbackとsanitized Free smoke成功後だけ。schedulerはinactiveを維持 |
| source-unpause-deletion | GO達成に必須ではなく、暗黙承認しない。原sourceはforensics用に保持 |
| activation | Gate 1外。Paid/Provider/Checkout/Cron activationを行わない |

commit/push/PR/mergeはreview済みsourceの統合のための独立した記録を持つ。CI未確認や必須local検証未完了のままsource readinessを受け入れない。backup/artifact削除はGate 1の前提ではなく、保持する。local試験の生成物だけは、そのrunのID・label・digest・mount・path所有確認後にcleanupする。

## Backup / restore / watchdog

### 固定版CLIのargument recipe

以下は2026-09-06にCLI 2.109.0とPG17.11の各`--help`でflagを確認した**非実行レシピ**。private linked targetの照合、Direct/TLS強制、sanitized captureを備えた実行producerは未完備であり、その完成前には実行しない。接続値を引数へ埋め込まず、raw stdout/stderrをterminalへ流さない。

| 段階 | CLI引数 | readback / stop |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 適用前一覧 | `migration list --linked` | bindingとlinked targetをprivateに照合し、全version/name行をfixtureと比較 |
| pending確認 | `db push --linked --dry-run --include-all` | Preview26 / Production34の期待完全一覧と一致。未知・重複・欠落は停止 |
| exact apply | `db push --linked --include-all` | source統合、一覧一致、環境別backup gate後に1回。seed/roles追加は禁止。失敗時は自動再試行しない |
| 適用後一覧 | `migration list --linked` | final56完全一致とpending空。別途fresh完全catalog/security readback必須 |
| filtering discovery | `db dump --linked --dry-run` | scriptはrestricted capture内で引数として検証し、実行可能textとして評価しない |
| Auth/Storage差分 | `db diff --linked --schema auth,storage --file <restricted-output-name>` | `--file`は新migration出力機能。repositoryで実行せず、専用restricted copied work directory内の生成先を確認。差分はreview後だけ利用 |
| roles | `db dump --linked --role-only --file <restricted>/roles.sql` | snapshot外。exit0、bytes、SHA-256を確認 |

`--include-all`は承認済み過去versionを含むexact集合のためだけであり、未知migrationを許可しない。`<restricted...>`は解決済み保存先の説明記号。`--linked`だけではDirect/TLSを証明できない。`--db-url`、password引数、`--debug`は禁止。`db diff`のshadow runtimeも専用生成物に限定し、既存runtime/volumeを流用しない。

snapshot dump本体はネイティブ`pg_dump`を使う。snapshot値は非表示pipeでrunnerへ渡す。

| artifact | PG17引数（非実行レシピ） |
| --- | --- |
| schema.sql | `--schema-only --snapshot <private-snapshot> --file <restricted>/schema.sql` + reviewed filters |
| data.sql | `--data-only --snapshot <private-snapshot> --file <restricted>/data.sql` + reviewed filters |
| history_schema.sql | `--schema-only --schema=supabase_migrations --snapshot <private-snapshot> --file <restricted>/history_schema.sql` |
| history_data.sql | `--data-only --schema=supabase_migrations --snapshot <private-snapshot> --file <restricted>/history_data.sql` |

COPYはPG17 `pg_dump`の既定。`--use-copy`はSupabase CLI専用でありネイティブへ渡さない。`--inserts`、`--column-inserts`、`--rows-per-insert`を拒否してCOPYを維持する。command-planのflag混入修正と直接回帰テストは親検証・再レビューで受入済み。これは実backup/restoreの成功ではなく、実行producer・接続・snapshot・復旧証拠は別途必要。

plain SQL restoreは下記の順に、新規専用targetで`psql --no-psqlrc --set=ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --single-transaction --file <restricted-artifact>`を配列呼出しし、各fileのexit0を要求する。失敗targetをreset/reuseして続行しない。`pg_restore --version`確認をplain SQLへの`pg_restore`実行に置き換えない。このレシピを実行済み証拠やhosted GOに昇格させない。

実行toolはPG major 17とCLI 2.109.0をpinし、CLIのdry-runから得たSupabase filtering argumentsをreviewする。command plan出力はbackup実行証拠ではない。[backup preflight](../../scripts/comment-translator-paid-core-v1-gate1-backup-recovery-preflight.ps1)の既定動作は非実行である。

- backup前にVault total/required-name rows=0、Storage objects=0をfresh確認する。非zeroなら既存設計では進めない。
- Auth/Storage差分はexact CLIの機能でread-only確認する。差分ありならreview済み `auth_storage_changes.sql` をrestricted artifactとして扱う。未reviewの差分は停止する。
- 必須artifactは `roles.sql`、`schema.sql`、COPY形式の`data.sql`、`history_schema.sql`、`history_data.sql`。本文、接続値、snapshot IDは表示・commitしない。filename/bytes/SHA-256/exit/timestampsのみ報告する。
- `T0`はsnapshot export元の同一REPEATABLE READ / READ ONLY transactionのserver時刻。schema/data/historyのdump完了までexport元transactionを保持する。rolesはsnapshot外の別取得として記録する。
- restore順は roles → schema → review済みAuth/Storage差分（ある場合）→ data → history schema → history data。対応toolでON_ERROR_STOPとtransactionを使用し、非対応modeまで単一transactionと主張しない。
- 新規local targetでhistory完全一致、legacy 3表0行、非Paid aggregate counts、Auth/FK/schema、bridge/canonical rehearsalを確認する。local restoreはhosted復旧枠の証明ではない。

| State / deadline | 必須動作 |
| --- | --- |
| PRE_DDL_UNARMED | backup/checksum中。pauseは禁止 |
| T0+5分まで未完了 | ABORTED_NO_DDL_NO_PAUSE。候補を復旧に使用せず、DDL/pauseなし。次回は新snapshot/T0 |
| ARMED_BEFORE_FIRST_DDL | backup/checksum完了後、最初のDDL直前にarm |
| T0+10分より前にmigration＋決定的readback成功 | SUCCESS_DISARMED。pause latchがあれば成功へ戻さない |
| arm後の決定的失敗／T0+10分まで未成功 | 即時、遅くともT0+10分にPAUSE_REQUESTED |
| T0+20分以内 | exact sourceのinaccessibilityを確認してPAUSE_CONFIRMED |
| deadline内のpause未確認 | T0 backupによるrestore/cutoverは禁止。NO-GO、証跡保持、review済みforward対応へ |

emergency recoveryはsource停止確認 → 無償復旧先のprovision/config → 所定順restore → history/data/Auth/Storage/grants/RLS/catalog/Vault readback → endpoint/credential cutover → client再認証 → Free smoke → writes再開。原sourceを自動unpause/deleteしない。Freeでのzero-loss rollbackは主張しない。

## Watchdog runner and recovery handoff (2026-09-08)

Local implementation: [watchdog runner](../../scripts/comment-translator-paid-core-v1-gate1-watchdog-runner.mjs), [deadline supervisor](../../scripts/lib/comment-translator-paid-core-v1-gate1-watchdog.mjs), [native transport](../../scripts/lib/comment-translator-paid-core-v1-gate1-watchdog-transport.mjs), [focused tests](../../scripts/comment-translator-paid-core-v1-gate1-watchdog.test.mjs). This implements execution, not operational GO. The CLI admits only native dependencies and rejects producer files whose actual bytes differ from its explicitly selected source commit. The new source is currently unpublished. The exported dependency seams are for local tests; results obtained through them are not native hosted authority.

Run the runner as a separate process using Node and its script path, with no credentials or JSON in argv. The trusted operator supplies bounded UTF8 NDJSON through private stdin. Sequence starts at0 and increases by exactly1. `init` carries policy (schemaVersion1, real snapshot-server T0, runId, sourceCommit, Production binding SHA), exact private binding, minimal libpq environment, Management API token, target/run/source-bound read/pre-authorized pause/confirmation permissions and an existing ignored journal directory below this checkout's `.tmp`. No secret or project identifier is emitted. The journal contains only sanitized metadata and a sequential hash chain, opened exclusively and fsynced. It is separate from the immutable six backup files.

Wait for `ready`, send `backup` with the exact six-file manifest/directory and producer completion/checksum timestamps, then wait for `backup-accepted`. The runner performs native persisted-byte/ACL inspection and requires completion, checksums, inspection and durable acknowledgement within T0+5 minutes. The supervising operator must independently bind that manifest and those timestamps to the native same-snapshot capture receipt; the runner's byte inspection alone cannot establish acquisition provenance or make an old rehearsal backup current. Send `arm` and wait for the durable `armed` acknowledgement immediately before starting DDL. No DDL may run after absent/failed acknowledgement. After actual migration and decisive catalog/security verification, send `success` with same-run/source/target identity and the actual ordered completion timestamps. These are reports from the trusted native executor, not a replacement for full readback evidence. On decisive failure send `failure`; retain the process/channel until its terminal receipt. EOF, malformed or out-of-order input after arm latches pause. Do not terminate the watchdog to cancel an armed run.

The separate process uses monotonic elapsed time plus wall-clock progress, with a one-second request scheduling margin before the10-minute deadline. Requests are one-shot and individually bounded. Request rejection/timeout does not imply a stopped project; confirmation continues independently. Lost time, failed durable records, late results and20-minute expiry cannot establish recovery eligibility. A process or machine outage is not proven safe by the local timer tests: the actual execution host, supervisor lifetime, native input/receipt bindings and emergency authority must be accepted before arming. A wake after expiry may finish its one bounded pause attempt, but cannot authorize restore. Even `SUCCESS_DISARMED` returns whole-Gate `NO-GO`; the separate nine-stage verifier remains mandatory.

Native pause uses the fixed [pause endpoint](https://supabase.com/docs/reference/api/v1-pause-a-project); project identity/status uses the fixed [project endpoint](https://supabase.com/docs/reference/api/v1-get-project). TLS is mandatory, redirects are rejected, and the token goes only to the Management API origin. Preflight requires exact ACTIVE_HEALTHY identity and a successful read-only PostgreSQL17 probe. The currently implemented confirmation oracle requires exact fresh INACTIVE identity, explicit connection refusal on the bound Direct database endpoint, and HTTP503 from both the bound REST and Auth endpoints. DNS errors, TLS errors, timeouts, pending statuses, generic non-success and authorization failures remain UNKNOWN. This oracle has local transport-contract coverage; its behavior on an actual paused hosted project has not been observed. Do not claim emergency readiness or relax it merely because the normal-state preflight succeeds.

The incident checklist below maps all ten required configuration entries to concrete consumers and stop conditions. Values remain in protected operator artifacts; a row's specification is not proof that the hosted setting has been applied.

| Entry | Exact preparation / incident action / verification |
| --- | --- |
| region | Preserve the user-selected same Free organization and `ap-northeast-1`. Recheck the zero-cost plan/quote and active limit before arm. Provision only after exact source inaccessibility is confirmed within20 minutes; healthy Production is never paused to create a rehearsal slot. Verify the new project's actual identity/region/PG17 before restore. |
| extensions | Compare the complete captured extension/version inventory and require the design's pg_net, pg_cron and Vault availability. Check managed Auth77/Storage68 compatibility and the explicitly bounded index/ACL differences. Perform the R5 empty-public default-privilege preparation as ordinary postgres, then restore the unchanged six files. Stop on an unknown platform baseline or permissions difference. |
| authProviders | Restore the protected Auth export's selected providers and settings; the saved selection is Email enabled with other providers disabled. Independently read back configuration. Auth table restoration is separate from provider configuration; do not create replacement user IDs. |
| authRedirects | Restore the exact protected Site URL/redirect list, including the application confirmation flow implemented in `app/auth/confirm/route.ts`. Compare saved values privately; never infer new callbacks or expose URL/private input lists in logs. |
| endpointCutover | Identify the actually deployed Production application artifact first; `wrangler.jsonc` names `v-streamer-tools`, but source configuration is not deployed identity. Prepare an emergency rebuild of that accepted production application revision with the Recovery public URL/key and server credentials. `lib/supabase/env.ts`, `server.ts`, `browser.ts`, billing runtime and durable stores consume these settings. Rebuild/redeploy is required for browser-bundled NEXT_PUBLIC values; a server-secret update alone is insufficient. Keep normal feature deployment/Paid activation outside this operation. |
| secretRotation | Replace the recovered project's `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` and actually consumed `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` together. `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY` is a declared server name, not a substitute for consumers that explicitly read service-role. Obtain new project credentials through protected input; do not copy a paused project's project-scoped credentials. Preserve the existing application-level YouTube credential-reference/token-store key references and versions needed to read restored records; `lib/comment-translator-youtube-oauth-token-store-persistence.ts` identifies `YOUTUBE_OAUTH_TOKEN_STORE_KEY_REF` / `_VERSION`. Do not blindly rotate these independent keys or OAuth/Stripe secrets. |
| clientReauthentication | Keep old project tokens/cookies out of the new session. Exercise the new-project login and `/auth/confirm` path with the same restored account identity. `app/account/actions.ts` supplies signOutAction, but remote sign-out against an unavailable old project is not proof of global invalidation. Verify old tokens fail and the new authenticated session is bound to Recovery; never inspect or log browser token values. |
| freeSmoke | With only the approved operator able to reach the recovery application, verify login/account, `/tools/comment-translator`, saved Free settings/quota projection and the approved bounded session Start/Stop path. `app/api/comment-translator/session/route.ts` is the route boundary. A live translation/provider/OAuth exercise requires its specific incident authorization and fixture; otherwise report that portion unverified. Confirm no Paid entitlement promotion, Checkout or maintenance execution. |
| reopenWrites | Keep an approved edge-level maintenance/access restriction until readback and operator smoke pass. The existing `COMMENT_TRANSLATOR_FREE_BETA_RUNTIME_ACCESS` / `COMMENT_TRANSLATOR_PRIVATE_LAUNCH_ALLOWED_USER_HASHES` gate in `lib/comment-translator-private-launch-access-gate.ts` can restrict Translator access, but does not cover every Auth/webhook/direct writer. Do not use it as a global write freeze. Exact edge rule/allowed operator, all writer coverage and reopening authority are still required; cutover remains blocked if they are absent. Restore the reviewed access configuration only after explicit writes-reopen approval. |
| exactSourcePause | Bind the source ref privately to the reviewed Production Direct binding. Retain the watchdog pause/confirmation receipt and independent complete history/data/Auth/grants/RLS/Vault/Storage readback. Keep the source paused; no automatic unpause or deletion. In `workers/comment-translator-paid-open-next-wrapper.mjs`, prevent `COMMENT_TRANSLATOR_PAID_SCHEDULER_AUTHORITY` from selecting `cloudflare-cron-fallback`; verify actual fallback state separately from the source guard and preserve inactive database Cron. |

Remaining operational inputs are explicit: trusted native backup/executor receipt binding, actual paused-project oracle support, accepted supervisor host/lifetime, deployed Production application identity, protected recovery credential references, complete edge restriction/writer coverage and the individual incident/cutover/reopening approvals. No existing local PASS or checklist hash fills these gaps. No source pause, project provisioning, Auth change, credential rewrite, deploy, restore or cutover was performed in this local cycle.

## Vault / inactive Cron

2026-09-06 PG17拒否境界訂正: `\quit 4/5`は終了コード指定として機能しないため使用禁止。reserved名の既存/partial拒否は固定SQLSTATE `PGT01`、commit前postcondition失敗は`PGT02`のSQL例外で現在transactionをabortし、`ON_ERROR_STOP=1`のpsql exit3で停止する。bound runner/local adapterには`VERBOSITY=sqlstate`を指定し、本文・直接メッセージを表示しない。local harnessはexit3と唯一の期待SQLSTATE ERROR recordを組で検証し、stdout0、有限capture、既存行保持または独立zero-row readbackを要求する。generic nonzero、別SQLSTATE、context内のトークン、複数/欠落ERROR recordは期待拒否として受理しない。second-createのtest-only注入例外は`PGT03`として同じ厳密判定を行い、故障タイミングは変えない。advisory lock、既存predicate、bound input、暗号化、成功時だけのCOMMIT、hosted readinessによる接続前blockは維持する。変更対象はVault runnerとlocal integration contractの2ファイルだけ。

2026-09-07 bound値忠実性訂正: PG17の同一adapter・新規隔離環境で、非秘密stress値と予約名の独立MD5比較を各形式1回実施した。SQLリテラル用の変数展開は両値不一致、raw変数展開は両値一致（各exit0/stderr0）だった。runnerとpartial fixtureのbind引数はraw変数展開へ統一し、SQL文字列連結やshell再評価へ置換しない。source回帰は旧形式を拒否する。actual-local成功caseでは実runnerから取り出した同じbind行と既存adapterを用い、引用符・改行・バックスラッシュ・空白を含む非秘密値の独立期待digestとのサーバー側一致を最初に要求する。結果は固定一致フラグのみ検証し、値・digest・decrypted secretは表示/取得しない。不一致時は書込み前停止する。既存4ケース・厳密出力/SQLSTATE・readback・cleanup・hosted接続前blockを維持し、success出力の外部形式は変更しない。

Vault予約名は `comment_translator_paid_maintenance_url` と `comment_translator_paid_cron_token` の2つ。deterministic transaction advisory lock下で両名の不在を再確認し、bound/session inputから原子的に作成する。commit前にcount=distinct count=2、encrypted record non-null、duplicate=0を確認する。作成・assertion失敗は両件rollback。decrypted値は取得しない。現在のhosted Writeはreadiness未検証で接続前blockを維持する。

actual-local試験は実runnerのtransaction生成を再利用し、成功2件、rerun拒否、partial-existingのID保持、second-create失敗とpost-create assertion失敗の独立rollback readbackを確認する。syntheticだけでは完了しない。既存DB/volumeを使わず、cached digest、network none、tmpfs、log-driver none、秘密非表示、所有確認cleanupを必要とする。

2026-09-06 local harness訂正: cached image既定の`postgres -D /etc/postgresql`を保持し、その後に既存のlogging保護設定を付ける。所有検証も同じ完全CMDを照合する。bare case DBへのextension導入ではimage primaryとACL初期状態が異なるため、4ケースを順次別々の新規owned containerのimage-initialized primary DBで実行する。第1環境は成功とrerun、第2はpartial-existing保持、第3はsecond-create rollback、第4はpost-create assertion rollback。各環境の開始前にreserved名不在と既存のmetadata/権限条件を確認し、終了ごとに所有確認cleanupとresidue確認を行う。失敗したら後続ケースを開始しない。既存runtimeのreuse/reset、DB clone、GRANT/ALTER ROLE/owner変更、adminによる通常secret transaction代替は禁止。runner SQL、stress input、期待値、security比較は変更しない。primary単独成功は全matrix成功へ昇格させない。

隔離local case DBのschema scaffoldと通常検証は`postgres`を維持する。superuserが必要なextension導入だけは、所有確認済み新規container内で既存`supabase_admin`の存在・login・superuser能力を確認して実行する。ロールへのgrant、ALTER ROLE、owner変更はしない。導入後のmetadata/readback/secret transactionは必ず`postgres`へ戻す。bootstrap能力不足ならSETUP_BLOCKEDとし、hosted実行の権限や接続方式へ流用しない。

Cronは予約名 `comment-translator-paid-maintenance`、cadence `*/5 * * * *`、承認済みprivate maintenance-from-Vault呼出しだけ。database/usernameはpostgres。1transaction内で不在確認 → schedule → commit前にactive=false → exact row確認を行う。直接cron.job更新、二重scheduler、activationは禁止。

commit前baselineと新job identityをprivateに束ね、commit後10分（2 cadence）の開始・終了でinactive、同じjobのbaseline後run delta=0を確認する。無関係な過去job履歴を混ぜない。matching=1、active=0、scheduler process=1、承認済みcommand digestとcadence一致、Cloudflare fallback absent/inactiveを実測する。

## Rollback / stop / GO監査

bridge commit前はtransaction失敗に伴うcatalog/historyの原子性を確認する。commit後にarchiveだけを戻してhistoryを残す操作は禁止。review済みforward修正か、期限内pauseが確認された復旧先restore/cutoverのみを使用する。Vault/Cronの値や業務行を直接書き換えてrollbackを模擬しない。

target/commit/pending/fingerprintの不一致、legacy非zero、source-era変更、完全manifest/ACL/RLS/raw digest/dependency不一致、backup/recovery未完備、advisorの新規in-scopeまたはhigh/critical問題、Vault重複、Cron active/run発生、fallback二重化、秘密表示の必要性は停止条件。失敗したmutationを自動再試行しない。原因と変更するscopeを確定してから実行条件を更新する。

GOには、review済みsourceのPreview統合merge、両環境final56と完全canonical/security一致、archive/source-era保持、実backup/rehearsal/final snapshot/watchdog/無償復旧能力、extensions利用可能、Vault exact2名、inactive Cron exact1件と10分run0、fallback無効、phase別rollback証拠がすべて必要。通常deployとactivationは未実施のままとする。未取得、未実装、stale、間接証拠、矛盾が1つでもあればNO-GO。
